Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The transfer variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 651 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 408 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_651(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 651. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^408(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a transfer algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 590. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 349/1.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 590 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the transfer structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 349. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
